# Minimal recovery dynamics panel v0

Status: PASS
Gates: 5590/5590

All 14 public worlds retain both programs. Table shows the refusal program.
Mean is unconditional; infinity includes paths that never reset.

| World | P(eventual reset) | Mean ticks to reset | P(live without reset at 30) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| open_noiseless | 0 | infinity | 1 |
| open_three_noiseless | 0 | infinity | 1 |
| sealed_noiseless | 0 | infinity | 1 |
| immutable_record | 0 | infinity | 1 |
| open_noise_1000_m1 | 1 | 2000 | 0.98510824 |
| open_noise_1000_m3 | 1 | 8004002000 | 1 |
| open_noise_100_m1 | 1 | 200 | 0.86038419 |
| open_noise_100_m3 | 1 | 8040200 | 0.99999652 |
| open_noise_10_m1 | 1 | 20 | 0.21463876 |
| open_noise_10_m3 | 1 | 8420 | 0.996673 |
| sealed_noise | 1 | 8420 | 0.996673 |
| broken_actuator | 0 | infinity | 1 |
| external_writer | 1 | 4 | 0.00017858209 |
| competing_death | 3/23 | infinity | 3.8330639e-05 |

Full exact tables, vector costs, all maximizing ties, and separate reset
mechanisms are in summary.json. Complete chains, short paths and rational
hitting-equation certificates are in evidence.json. Source hashes are in
provenance.json. Reset persistence is assumed by absorbing reset states.

This is an instrument and analytic audit of declared mechanisms. It does
not establish a generic recovery floor or real-world recovery times.
